# 010100_Readme_Foundation_Static_Catalog_Package.md

## Purpose

This folder defines the foundation static catalog package, product-line static registry, static authorization, and static coding deferral boundaries.

## Folder-Owned Number Range

- Folder: `docs/010000_runtime_foundation_and_cross_room_architecture/010100_foundation_static_catalog_package/`
- Owned range: `010100~010199`
- Next sibling: `010200_static_catalog_runtime_planning/`

## Scope

- Foundation static catalog package.
- Product line capability registry.
- Static artifact authorization and deferral controls.

## Out Of Scope

- Store runtime room boundaries.
- Status catalog runtime planning.
- Runtime implementation.

# 010106_Policy_Foundation_Static_Catalog_Package_Closure_Runtime_Entry_Deferral.md

## Purpose

This document defines the Foundation Static Catalog Package Closure and Runtime Entry Deferral Policy.

The previous artifact `09990` defined the AI pgvector Governance Catalog Static Package Handoff and Non Authority Boundary Policy.

This document closes the `09900` static package planning sequence and defines how the first wave of static catalog candidates should be treated as a Foundation catalog layer before any runtime implementation, database mutation, provider integration, AI/pgvector execution, customer-visible publication, or value-bearing action begins.

The purpose is to prevent the static catalog wave from being mistaken for completed runtime implementation.

This document is planning-only.

It does not authorize coding.

---

## 2. Scope

This policy applies to the Foundation static catalog candidate wave covering:

1. Static security monitoring catalog registry
2. Static boundary test matrix
3. Static provider evidence registry
4. Static i18n message key registry
5. Static Catch Menu status catalog
6. Static Catch & Order status catalog
7. Static Support/Admin boundary catalog
8. Static recovery/compensation catalog
9. Static AI/pgvector governance catalog
10. Future static archive/legal hold catalog
11. Future static Franchise OS policy inheritance catalog
12. Future static incident learning catalog
13. Future explicit coding authorization packets
14. Future runtime entry gates
15. Future catalog validation procedures

This document does not implement any static files, catalogs, database tables, tests, provider calls, UI, runtime logic, AI runtime, pgvector ingestion, compensation execution, or deployment.

---

## 3. Core Principle

Static catalog closure is not runtime readiness.

The correct rule is:

Cataloged does not mean implemented.
Mapped does not mean enforced.
Reviewed does not mean deployed.
Referenced does not mean authorized.
Static validation does not mean runtime safety.
Runtime entry requires a separate gate.

The static catalog wave exists to create vocabulary, boundaries, blockers, and traceability before implementation.

---

## 4. Catalog Header

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Document ID | `10000` |
| Package ID | `foundation_static_catalog_package_closure.runtime_entry_deferral.v1` |
| Artifact Type | `STATIC_CATALOG_CLOSURE_POLICY` |
| Version | `v1` |
| Planning Status | `STATIC_CATALOG_WAVE_CLOSURE` |
| Coding Status | `CODING_DEFERRED` |
| Runtime Authority | `RUNTIME_ENTRY_NOT_AUTHORIZED` |
| Owner | `Product / Security / QA / Engineering / Support / Finance / Legal / AI Governance / Data Governance` |
| Dependencies | `09560` to `09990` |
| Provider Evidence Status | `REFERENCE_ONLY_UNTIL_SEPARATE_RUNTIME_GATE` |
| i18n Requirement | `REFERENCE_ONLY_UNTIL_SEPARATE_PUBLICATION_GATE` |
| Audit Requirement | `IMPLEMENTATION_DECISION_AUDIT_REQUIRED_IF_CODED_LATER` |
| Security Requirement | `STATIC_TO_RUNTIME_SEPARATION_REQUIRED` |
| Review Requirement | `FOUNDATION_CATALOG_WAVE_REVIEW_REQUIRED` |
| Blocker Status | `RUNTIME_ENTRY_BLOCKED_BY_DEFAULT` |

---

## 5. Static Package Wave Summary

The static package wave includes the following candidate packages:

| Document | Candidate Package | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `09910` | `security_monitoring_catalog_registry_static_v1` | Foundation registry |
| `09920` | `boundary_test_matrix_static_v1` | Boundary test vocabulary |
| `09930` | `provider_evidence_registry_static_v1` | Provider evidence traceability |
| `09940` | `i18n_message_key_registry_static_v1` | Message key and locale review |
| `09950` | `catch_menu_status_catalog_static_v1` | Catch Menu safe status catalog |
| `09960` | `catch_order_status_catalog_static_v1` | Catch & Order safe status catalog |
| `09970` | `support_admin_boundary_catalog_static_v1` | Support/admin authority boundary |
| `09980` | `recovery_compensation_catalog_static_v1` | Recovery/value authority catalog |
| `09990` | `ai_pgvector_governance_catalog_static_v1` | AI/vector non-authority catalog |

All packages remain static and non-runtime unless separately authorized.

---

## 6. Static Wave Closure Definition

Static wave closure means:

- candidate packages have been identified
- scopes and non-scopes have been declared
- catalog families have been defined
- record schemas have been drafted
- validation checklists have been drafted
- rollback patterns have been drafted
- blockers have been declared
- runtime entry remains deferred

Static wave closure does not mean:

- files have been created
- validation has been executed
- runtime has been enabled
- customer messages have been published
- provider capability has been verified
- AI or pgvector is active
- compensation can execute
- support/admin workflow exists

Closure is a planning milestone only.

---

## 7. Runtime Entry Deferral Rule

Runtime entry must remain blocked for:

1. Provider integrations
2. POS handoff execution
3. Payment execution
4. KDS routing execution
5. Catch Menu customer UI
6. Catch & Order customer UI
7. Customer message publication
8. Support/admin workflow
9. Recovery/compensation workflow
10. Coupon/point/wallet/refund action
11. AI runtime
12. pgvector ingestion or retrieval
13. Archive restore/delete
14. Legal hold mutation
15. Franchise OS policy engine
16. Mass recovery workflow
17. Security daemon runtime
18. Database triggers/functions
19. Production deployment

A separate runtime entry gate is required.

---

## 8. Static Catalog Acceptance Gate

A static catalog package may be accepted for planning only if:

- candidate id exists
- package name exists
- source documents are listed
- scope is locked
- non-scope is locked
- record schema exists
- controlled status catalog exists
- validation checklist exists
- rollback plan exists
- blockers are explicit
- runtime use status defaults to not authorized
- no secrets are included
- no customer/payment/raw provider data is included
- no runtime behavior is introduced

Acceptance for planning is not coding authorization.

---

## 9. Static Catalog Coding Gate

If static catalog coding is later authorized, the authorization must include:

| Field | Required Meaning |
|---|---|
| `candidate_id` | Exact candidate id |
| `package_name` | Exact package name |
| `allowed_operations` | Static file/catalog creation only |
| `prohibited_operations` | Runtime, provider, payment, POS, KDS, AI/vector, value action |
| `target_paths` | Exact paths |
| `file_format` | Exact format |
| `validation_method` | Manual or command-based validation |
| `rollback_plan` | Exact rollback |
| `reviewers` | Required reviewers |
| `decision` | `CODING_ALLOWED_NARROW_SCOPE` only |
| `expiration` | Optional time limit |

Without this packet, coding remains unauthorized.

---

## 10. Runtime Entry Gate

Runtime entry requires a separate package that must declare:

- runtime domain
- source catalogs
- source tests
- provider evidence dependency
- i18n dependency
- data model
- API/RPC/event boundary
- authority boundary
- audit/evidence capture
- error code mapping
- rollback/fallback
- deployment boundary
- monitoring boundary
- support runbook
- customer communication boundary
- security review
- QA review
- legal/finance review where applicable
- explicit runtime decision

No runtime package may rely on static catalog existence alone.

---

## 11. Static To Runtime Traceability Rule

Every runtime candidate must trace back to:

- static registry records
- boundary test records
- provider evidence records if provider-related
- i18n message keys if visible text related
- support/admin boundaries if review-surface related
- recovery/compensation catalog if value-related
- AI/pgvector governance if AI/vector related
- archive/legal hold policy if retention/evidence related
- Franchise OS policy if tenant/store/franchise related

If traceability is missing, runtime entry is blocked.

---

## 12. Blocking Invariants

The following invariants must remain blocking:

| Invariant | Runtime Impact |
|---|---|
| `ACKNOWLEDGED_NOT_RESOLVED` | Cannot close from acknowledgement |
| `EVIDENCE_NOT_APPROVAL` | Cannot approve from evidence alone |
| `PROJECTION_NOT_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH` | Cannot treat projection as truth |
| `PROVIDER_STATE_NOT_INTERNAL_TRUTH` | Cannot trust provider state without evidence |
| `AI_NOT_AUTHORITY` | Cannot allow AI to approve/mutate/send/close |
| `PGVECTOR_NOT_PROOF` | Cannot use similarity as proof |
| `RESTORE_NOT_MUTATION` | Cannot restore archive as runtime mutation |
| `CONTAINMENT_NOT_RESOLUTION` | Cannot treat containment as resolved |
| `QUARANTINE_NOT_DELETION` | Cannot treat quarantine as deletion |
| `CALLBACK_NOT_VERIFIED_STATE` | Cannot trust callback without verification |
| `POS_ACCEPTED_NOT_PAYMENT_CONFIRMED` | Cannot map POS accepted to payment confirmed |
| `KDS_COMPLETED_NOT_SETTLED` | Cannot map KDS completed to settlement |

Any runtime design violating these invariants is blocked.

---

## 13. Provider Runtime Deferral

Provider runtime remains deferred until:

- provider evidence registry is accepted
- provider capability evidence is reviewed
- sandbox/production distinction is clear
- callback verification is defined
- idempotency and replay behavior are defined
- error mapping is defined
- boundary tests are mapped
- customer-visible capability claims are reviewed
- rollback/fallback is defined

Provider marketing or documentation alone is not enough.

---

## 14. Customer Message Runtime Deferral

Customer message runtime remains deferred until:

- i18n key exists
- fallback key exists
- locale review status is complete
- customer promise boundary is reviewed
- provider/payment/value/security wording is safe
- legal/finance review is complete where needed
- publication channel is defined
- rollback/correction notice path exists
- audit is defined

Static keys do not publish messages.

---

## 15. Catch Menu Runtime Deferral

Catch Menu runtime remains deferred until:

- status catalog is accepted
- customer-safe state mapping is complete
- QR/NFC entry boundary is defined
- menu projection source is defined
- availability/price/allergen review is defined
- provider dependency is reviewed
- i18n keys are accepted
- fallback behavior is defined
- boundary tests are mapped

Catch Menu must remain customer-safe and non-authoritative.

---

## 16. Catch & Order Runtime Deferral

Catch & Order runtime remains deferred until:

- status catalog is accepted
- order/POS/payment/KDS boundaries are mapped
- provider evidence is reviewed
- payment uncertainty messaging is reviewed
- idempotency is designed
- duplicate prevention is designed
- support/staff review routing is defined
- audit/evidence capture is defined
- rollback/fallback is defined

Catch & Order must not collapse order, POS, payment, and KDS truth into one state.

---

## 17. Support Admin Runtime Deferral

Support/Admin runtime remains deferred until:

- boundary catalog is accepted
- visibility levels are defined
- allowed/prohibited actions are enforced
- masking/unmasking is designed
- escalation routes are defined
- customer send boundary is reviewed
- AI/pgvector labels are defined
- audit is defined
- authority separation is testable

Support visibility must not become hidden mutation authority.

---

## 18. Recovery Compensation Runtime Deferral

Recovery/compensation runtime remains deferred until:

- recovery/compensation catalog is accepted
- authority levels are mapped
- evidence requirements are enforced
- idempotency is designed
- reconciliation is designed
- rollback/reversal is designed
- high-risk escalation is enforced
- customer message boundary is reviewed
- finance/legal/security review gates are defined

Value action requires stricter runtime gate.

---

## 19. AI pgvector Runtime Deferral

AI/pgvector runtime remains deferred until:

- AI governance catalog is accepted
- pgvector source catalog is accepted
- source approval is complete
- sensitive data exclusion is defined
- traceability is defined
- similarity warning is enforced
- customer visibility is blocked unless reviewed
- human review boundary is enforced
- audit is defined
- boundary tests are mapped

AI and pgvector must remain advisory.

---

## 20. Archive Legal Hold Runtime Deferral

Archive/legal hold runtime remains deferred until:

- archive retention catalog exists
- legal hold rule exists
- restore boundary is defined
- deletion/anonymization boundary is defined
- evidence preservation is defined
- access logging is defined
- support/admin authority is limited
- audit is immutable
- rollback does not erase original evidence

Archive and legal hold protect evidence integrity.

---

## 21. Franchise OS Runtime Deferral

Franchise OS policy runtime remains deferred until:

- policy layer catalog exists
- HQ ceiling is defined
- tenant/franchise/owner/store layers are defined
- legal/security/finance precedence is enforced
- local override boundary is defined
- policy exception audit is defined
- customer message consistency is defined
- compensation authority inheritance is defined
- boundary tests are mapped

Franchise OS must not allow uncontrolled local override.

---

## 22. Closure Output

The expected closure output of this static wave is not code.

The expected output is:

- package list
- candidate ids
- source references
- scope/non-scope definitions
- record schema drafts
- status catalogs
- validation checklists
- rollback patterns
- blocker lists
- runtime deferral gates
- next authorization requirements

This closure output prepares controlled implementation.

It does not execute it.

---

## 23. Next Recommended Planning Package

The next recommended planning package after this closure is:

`10010 Explicit Static Catalog Coding Authorization Packet Template`

Purpose:

- define the exact structure of a narrow coding authorization
- list required target paths
- list required file formats
- list validation requirements
- list rollback requirements
- separate authorization from actual execution

This remains planning unless the user explicitly approves coding.

---

## 24. Static Wave Anti-Pattern List

Avoid:

- treating static catalog as runtime
- creating database tables without approval
- adding runtime validation while creating catalogs
- publishing messages from i18n keys
- calling providers from provider registry
- executing AI from AI governance catalog
- ingesting vectors from pgvector catalog
- issuing compensation from recovery catalog
- exposing support actions from support boundary catalog
- using Catch Menu status as operational truth
- using Catch & Order status as payment truth
- allowing Franchise OS local override before policy engine review

These anti-patterns must block runtime.

---

## 25. Validation Checklist

Validation must confirm:

1. Static package wave list exists.
2. Every package has candidate id.
3. Every package has scope and non-scope.
4. Every package defaults runtime use to not authorized.
5. Every package has validation checklist.
6. Every package has rollback plan.
7. Every package has blockers.
8. Runtime entry is explicitly deferred.
9. Provider runtime deferral exists.
10. Customer message runtime deferral exists.
11. Catch Menu runtime deferral exists.
12. Catch & Order runtime deferral exists.
13. Support/Admin runtime deferral exists.
14. Recovery/compensation runtime deferral exists.
15. AI/pgvector runtime deferral exists.
16. Archive/legal hold runtime deferral exists.
17. Franchise OS runtime deferral exists.
18. Static-to-runtime traceability rule exists.
19. Blocking invariants exist.
20. Next authorization package is identified.
21. Coding remains deferred.

---

## 28. Final Rule

The Foundation static catalog wave is closed for planning when candidate packages, scopes, non-scopes, schemas, validation checklists, rollback plans, blockers, invariants, and runtime deferral gates are documented.

Static catalog closure does not authorize coding or runtime use.

No provider integration, payment/POS/KDS execution, customer-visible publication, support/admin workflow, value action, AI runtime, pgvector ingestion/retrieval, archive/legal mutation, Franchise OS policy engine, database trigger/function, or production deployment may proceed from static catalog closure alone.

The next step must be a separate explicit authorization packet that grants `CODING_ALLOWED_NARROW_SCOPE` for a specific static package, with target paths, format, validation, rollback, reviewers, blockers, and prohibited operations declared.

===== BEGIN docs/010000_runtime_foundation_and_cross_room_architecture/010100_foundation_static_catalog_package/010110_Policy_Explicit_Static_Catalog_Coding_Authorization_Packet_Template_And_Approval_Boundary.md =====
# 010110_Policy_Explicit_Static_Catalog_Coding_Authorization_Packet_Template_And_Approval_Boundary.md

## Purpose

This document defines the Explicit Static Catalog Coding Authorization Packet Template and Approval Boundary Policy.

The previous artifact `10000` defined the Foundation Static Catalog Package Closure and Runtime Entry Deferral Policy.

This document defines the exact structure required before any static catalog package may move from planning into narrow coding.

The purpose is to prevent vague approval, accidental runtime expansion, hidden provider integration, customer-visible publication, database mutation, AI/vector execution, support/admin workflow creation, value-bearing action, or production deployment during static catalog implementation.

This document is an authorization template policy.

It does not authorize coding by itself.

---

## 2. Scope

This policy applies to explicit coding authorization packets for:

1. Static security monitoring catalog registry
2. Static boundary test matrix
3. Static provider evidence registry
4. Static i18n message key registry
5. Static Catch Menu status catalog
6. Static Catch & Order status catalog
7. Static Support/Admin boundary catalog
8. Static recovery/compensation catalog
9. Static AI/pgvector governance catalog
10. Future static archive/legal hold catalog
11. Future static Franchise OS policy inheritance catalog
12. Future static incident learning catalog
13. Future static implementation candidate records
14. Future static validation checklist records
15. Future static catalog index patches

This policy does not authorize runtime implementation.

---

## 3. Core Principle

Coding authorization must be explicit, narrow, and revocable.

The correct rule is:

No implied coding.
No broad coding.
No runtime coding hidden inside catalog work.
No provider calls.
No database mutation unless separately approved.
No customer-visible publication.
No AI/vector runtime.
No value action.
No production deployment.

A static catalog coding authorization packet must say exactly what is allowed and exactly what is prohibited.

---

## 4. Catalog Header

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Document ID | `10010` |
| Package ID | `explicit_static_catalog_coding_authorization_packet_template.v1` |
| Artifact Type | `CODING_AUTHORIZATION_TEMPLATE_POLICY` |
| Version | `v1` |
| Planning Status | `AUTHORIZATION_TEMPLATE_DRAFT` |
| Coding Status | `CODING_DEFERRED` |
| Runtime Authority | `RUNTIME_ENTRY_NOT_AUTHORIZED` |
| Owner | `Product / Security / QA / Engineering` |
| Dependencies | `09560` to `10000` |
| Provider Evidence Status | `REFERENCE_ONLY_UNTIL_PACKAGE_SPECIFIES` |
| i18n Requirement | `REFERENCE_ONLY_UNTIL_PACKAGE_SPECIFIES` |
| Audit Requirement | `CODING_DECISION_AUDIT_REQUIRED` |
| Security Requirement | `EXPLICIT_SCOPE_AND_NON_SCOPE_REQUIRED` |
| Review Requirement | `PRODUCT_SECURITY_QA_ENGINEERING_REVIEW_REQUIRED` |
| Blocker Status | `AUTHORIZATION_PACKET_REQUIRED_BEFORE_CODING` |

---

## 5. Authorization Packet Definition

An Explicit Static Catalog Coding Authorization Packet is a short, controlled document that grants narrow permission to create or modify static catalog artifacts.

It must include:

- candidate id
- package name
- source policy documents
- exact target paths
- exact file formats
- allowed operations
- prohibited operations
- validation method
- rollback method
- reviewer list
- blocker resolution
- expiration or scope limit
- final decision

It must not contain vague permission such as:

- “Proceed with implementation.”
- “Create the needed files.”
- “Start coding the package.”
- “Build the system.”
- “Wire it up.”
- “Make it work.”

Authorization must be precise.

---

## 6. Authorization Packet Template

A future authorization packet should use the following structure:

    Static Catalog Coding Authorization Packet:
    <authorization_id>

    Candidate ID:
    <candidate_id>

    Package Name:
    <package_name>

    Source Documents:
    <document ids and titles>

    Authorization Type:
    CODING_ALLOWED_NARROW_SCOPE

    Allowed Operations:
    <exact allowed operations>

    Prohibited Operations:
    <exact prohibited operations>

    Target Paths:
    <exact paths>

    File Format:
    <exact file formats>

    Required Files:
    <required files or records>

    Validation Method:
    <manual checklist or command>

    Rollback Plan:
    <exact rollback plan>

    Reviewers:
    <required reviewers>

    Blockers:
    <resolved or deferred blockers>

    Runtime Use Status:
    <must remain not authorized>

    Expiration:
    <optional expiration or one-shot scope>

    Final Decision:
    CODING_ALLOWED_NARROW_SCOPE

    Notes:
    <constraints>

This template grants coding only when filled, reviewed, and explicitly approved.

---

## 7. Authorization ID Pattern

Recommended authorization id pattern:

`AUTH-STATIC-<PACKAGE>-<NUMBER>`

Examples:

| Authorization ID | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `AUTH-STATIC-REGISTRY-0001` | Static registry authorization |
| `AUTH-STATIC-BOUNDARY-0001` | Static boundary test authorization |
| `AUTH-STATIC-PROVIDER-0001` | Static provider evidence authorization |
| `AUTH-STATIC-I18N-0001` | Static i18n authorization |
| `AUTH-STATIC-CATCH-MENU-0001` | Static Catch Menu catalog authorization |
| `AUTH-STATIC-CATCH-ORDER-0001` | Static Catch & Order catalog authorization |
| `AUTH-STATIC-SUPPORT-0001` | Static support/admin catalog authorization |
| `AUTH-STATIC-RECOVERY-0001` | Static recovery/compensation catalog authorization |
| `AUTH-STATIC-AI-VECTOR-0001` | Static AI/pgvector catalog authorization |

Authorization ids must be stable once referenced.

---

## 8. Allowed Authorization Types

Allowed authorization types:

| Authorization Type | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `CODING_ALLOWED_DOC_PATCH_ONLY` | Documentation patch only |
| `CODING_ALLOWED_STATIC_CATALOG_ONLY` | Static catalog files only |
| `CODING_ALLOWED_STATIC_INDEX_ONLY` | Static index/README only |
| `CODING_ALLOWED_STATIC_VALIDATION_ONLY` | Static validation checklist only |
| `CODING_ALLOWED_TEST_MATRIX_STATIC_ONLY` | Static test matrix only |
| `CODING_ALLOWED_NARROW_SCOPE` | Narrow defined scope only |

Disallowed in this policy:

| Authorization Type | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `CODING_ALLOWED_RUNTIME` | Not allowed here |
| `CODING_ALLOWED_PROVIDER_INTEGRATION` | Not allowed here |
| `CODING_ALLOWED_PAYMENT_FLOW` | Not allowed here |
| `CODING_ALLOWED_AI_RUNTIME` | Not allowed here |
| `CODING_ALLOWED_VECTOR_INGESTION` | Not allowed here |
| `CODING_ALLOWED_PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT` | Not allowed here |

This policy only covers static catalog authorization.

---

## 9. Required Allowed Operations Field

Allowed operations must be concrete.

Examples of acceptable allowed operations:

- create static JSON catalog file
- create static Markdown index
- create static validation checklist
- add source document references
- add controlled status values
- add candidate record document
- update README with static reference
- mark runtime use as not authorized
- add blocker ids
- add rollback notes

Allowed operations must not include runtime behavior.

---

## 10. Required Prohibited Operations Field

Every authorization packet must explicitly prohibit:

1. Runtime enforcement
2. Provider API calls
3. Payment operations
4. POS/KDS operations
5. Customer UI rendering
6. Customer message publication
7. Support/admin workflow execution
8. Refund/coupon/point/wallet action
9. Database trigger/function creation
10. AI model calls
11. Embedding generation
12. pgvector ingestion or retrieval
13. Archive restore/delete
14. Legal hold mutation
15. Franchise OS policy engine execution
16. Production deployment

If prohibited operations are missing, authorization is invalid.

---

## 11. Target Path Rule

Target paths must be explicit.

A packet must not say:

- “under catalogs”
- “where appropriate”
- “in the right folder”
- “according to structure”
- “create as needed”

A packet must say exact path candidates such as:

- `catalogs/foundation/security_monitoring/registry_index.md`
- `catalogs/foundation/security_monitoring/registry_records.json`
- `catalogs/foundation/boundary_tests/test_records.json`
- `catalogs/foundation/provider_evidence/provider_records.json`
- `catalogs/foundation/i18n/message_key_records.json`

No path, no coding.

---

## 12. File Format Rule

File format must be explicit.

Allowed static formats may include:

| Format | Condition |
|---|---|
| `Markdown` | Human-readable index/checklist |
| `JSON` | Structured static catalog |
| `YAML` | Human-editable static catalog if approved |
| `CSV` | Review spreadsheet if approved |
| `TXT` | Not preferred except simple notes |
| `SQL` | Not allowed under this static policy unless separately approved |
| `Database Table` | Not allowed under this static policy |

If format is unclear, coding is blocked.

---

## 13. Required Runtime Use Status

Every static artifact created under this authorization class must carry or imply:

`RUNTIME_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED`

Domain-specific equivalents may include:

- `REGISTRY_RUNTIME_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED`
- `TEST_RUNTIME_NOT_AUTHORIZED`
- `PROVIDER_RUNTIME_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED`
- `MESSAGE_RUNTIME_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED`
- `CATCH_MENU_RUNTIME_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED`
- `CATCH_ORDER_RUNTIME_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED`
- `SUPPORT_ADMIN_RUNTIME_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED`
- `RECOVERY_COMPENSATION_RUNTIME_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED`
- `AI_RUNTIME_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED`
- `PGVECTOR_RUNTIME_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED`

Runtime use status must not be omitted.

---

## 14. Validation Method Rule

Validation method must be declared before coding.

It may be:

- manual checklist
- schema validation
- duplicate id check
- source reference check
- status value check
- forbidden data check
- no-secrets check
- no-runtime-claim check
- no-customer-data check
- no-provider-payload check

If no validation method exists, coding remains blocked.

---

## 15. Rollback Plan Rule

Rollback must be simple and explicit.

Allowed rollback examples:

- revert added files
- revert index patch
- mark incorrect static record deprecated
- restore previous static file
- add blocker to downstream package
- remove invalid reference

Rollback must not require:

- production data repair
- customer notification
- provider correction
- payment correction
- vector deletion
- AI shutdown
- legal hold restoration

If rollback requires runtime correction, the package is not static.

---

## 16. Reviewer Rule

Minimum reviewers for static catalog authorization:

| Reviewer | Required For |
|---|---|
| Product | All packages |
| Security | All security/boundary/provider/support/value/AI packages |
| QA | All validation-bearing packages |
| Engineering | All file/layout/format packages |
| Support | Support/recovery/customer-message packages |
| i18n/Content | Message/Catch Menu/Catch & Order packages |
| Finance | Payment/value/compensation packages |
| Legal | Privacy/legal/allergen/high-risk message packages |
| Provider Ops | Provider-related packages |
| AI Governance | AI-related packages |
| Data Governance | pgvector/archive/data-source packages |
| Franchise Ops | Franchise policy packages |

Reviewer route must match package risk.

---

## 17. Blocker Resolution Rule

Authorization packet must classify blockers as:

| Blocker Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `BLOCKER_RESOLVED` | Resolved |
| `BLOCKER_DEFERRED_WITH_REASON` | Deferred with reason |
| `BLOCKER_NOT_APPLICABLE_WITH_REASON` | Not applicable |
| `BLOCKER_OPEN` | Open |
| `BLOCKER_BLOCKS_CODING` | Blocks coding |

If any blocker is `BLOCKER_OPEN` or `BLOCKER_BLOCKS_CODING`, final decision cannot be `CODING_ALLOWED_NARROW_SCOPE`.

---

## 18. Expiration Rule

A static catalog authorization may include expiration.

Examples:

- valid for one execution only
- valid until target paths are created
- valid until file format changes
- valid until source docs change
- valid until blocker changes
- valid for a named package only

Authorization must not become permanent open-ended coding permission.

---

## 19. No Scope Drift Rule

If coding reveals that new scope is needed, the implementer must stop.

Examples requiring stop:

- need to add database table
- need to add runtime loader
- need to run validation through app code
- need provider data
- need customer data
- need AI output
- need vector ingestion
- need support/admin UI
- need message publication
- need compensation execution

New scope requires a new authorization packet.

---

## 20. Authorization Review Checklist

Before approving a static catalog coding packet, verify:

1. Candidate id is exact.
2. Package name is exact.
3. Source documents are listed.
4. Authorization type is allowed.
5. Allowed operations are concrete.
6. Prohibited operations are explicit.
7. Target paths are exact.
8. File format is explicit.
9. Required files are listed.
10. Validation method exists.
11. Rollback plan exists.
12. Runtime use status remains not authorized.
13. Reviewers are listed.
14. Blockers are resolved or deferred with reason.
15. Scope drift rule is included.
16. Final decision is explicit.
17. No runtime authority is granted.

Failure blocks authorization.

---

    CAND-09910-STATIC-REGISTRY-001
